**Error 1 — Playbook Engine Missing Playbook as Structure + Liquidity + Volume + Volatility + FVG + OrderBlock + ICT/SMC/RTM + Wyckoff + Trend + Momentum Divergence + Regime + Session/Temporal — 12 Engines — But Missing Exact Playbook Logic:** Original playbook engine missing exact playbook logic: Playbook = f(Setup, Context Fabric, Cross-Domain, Quality Vector, Snapshot PIT) — Playbook Types: Breakout, Reversal, Continuation, Range, Liquidity Grab, FVG Fill, OrderBlock Mitigation, ICT Killzone, RTM Base, Wyckoff Spring/Test/SOS, Trend Pullback, Momentum Divergence, Regime Change, Session Open — Each Playbook with entry, stop, target, invalidation, confirmation, risk, reward, winrate, expected value, Q — Fixed with exact playbook logic with entry = f(Setup direction, Pattern, Structure BOS, Liquidity Sweep, FVG, OrderBlock, Killzone, Session) — Stop = f(Structure swing, ATR, volatility_regime, session_range, Q_window, Q_regime) — Target = f(ATR, session_range, liquidity, FVG, OrderBlock, trend_regime, volatility_regime, Q_forecast) — Invalidation = f(BOS opposite, Volume spike opposite, OI spike opposite, Q_window min<Q_thr, conflict_penalty>0.5, MTF_state CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT, market_regime Volatile Q_regime<0.5) — Confirmation = f(VolumeZ, VolRatio, OI_z, Displacement, BreakMag, BodyRatio, close_position, volume_ratio, normalized_range, Return_k, session, killzone, news_event) — Risk = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, regime uncertainty, volatility_regime, trend_regime, session_range, killzone, news_event, ATR, session, day_of_week, time_of_day) — Reward = f(Risk, target, stop, winrate, expected value, Q_forecast, Q_param) — Winrate = f(historical winrate, Q_forecast, Q_param, regime, session, killzone, news_event) — Expected Value = winrate*reward - (1-winrate)*risk — Q = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, Q_regime, regime_uncertainty, conflict_penalty, redundancy_penalty, MTF_state, market_regime, session_state, volatility_regime, trend_regime).

**Error 2 — Strategy Engine Missing StrategyProposal as f(Setup, Playbook, Forecast, Risk Kernel, Quality Vector, Snapshot PIT) — But Missing Exact Strategy Logic:** Original strategy engine missing exact strategy logic: StrategyProposal = f(Setup, Playbook, Forecast, Risk Kernel, Quality Vector, Snapshot PIT) — Strategy Types: Aggressive, Balanced, Conservative — L2 Profile Aggressive/Balanced/Conservative — Aggressive ε_price*0.5 ε_volume*0.5 Q_min=0.4 Q_thr[1m]=0.5 freshness_threshold*0.8 — Balanced ε_price*1.0 ε_volume*1.0 Q_min=0.5 Q_thr[1m]=0.6 freshness_threshold*1.0 — Conservative ε_price*2.0 ε_volume*2.0 Q_min=0.6 Q_thr[1m]=0.7 freshness_threshold*1.2 — Precision same — Fixed with exact strategy logic with Aggressive = high risk high reward low Q_min high winrate low — Balanced = medium risk medium reward medium Q_min medium winrate medium — Conservative = low risk low reward high Q_min high winrate high — StrategyProposal BLOCK if Setup BLOCK or Playbook BLOCK or Forecast BLOCK or Risk Kernel BLOCK or Q_window min<Q_thr or Q_forecast<0.5 or Q_param<0.5 or conflict_penalty>0.5 or redundancy_penalty>0.3 or MTF_state CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT or market_regime Volatile Q_regime<0.5 or session_state News high impact or volatility_regime Volatile Q_forecast<0.5 or trend_regime Breakout Q_forecast<0.5 or Hard 13 gates fail → QUARANTINED.

**Error 3 — Forecast Engine Missing Forecast P/U/C — Probability/Uncertainty/Calibration — But Missing Exact Forecast Logic:** Original forecast engine missing exact forecast logic: Forecast P/U/C — Probability = f(Setup, Playbook, Strategy, Quality Vector, Snapshot PIT, Cross-Domain, Context Fabric, Pattern Engine, MarketObservation, CandleFeature, EvidenceEvent) — Uncertainty = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, regime uncertainty, volatility_regime, trend_regime, session_range, killzone, news_event, ATR, session, day_of_week, time_of_day) — Calibration = f(rolling_calibration_error, Brier, log_loss) — rolling_calibration_error = |predicted_prob - observed_freq| over 100 samples — rolling window 100 — If drift>tol → Q_forecast down — Brier = mean((prob - outcome)^2) — Brier ∈ [0,1] — log_loss = -mean(outcome*log(prob)+(1-outcome)*log(1-prob)) — log_loss ∈ [0,∞) — Q_forecast = 1 - rolling_calibration_error - Brier - log_loss — Q_forecast ∈ [0,1] — If Q_forecast<0.5 → Forecast BLOCK — Portfolio BLOCK — Risk Budget down — TradePlan BLOCK — Fixed with exact forecast logic with Probability = f(Setup final_score, Playbook winrate, Strategy expected value, Quality Vector Q_window Q_forecast Q_param Q_regime regime_uncertainty conflict_penalty redundancy_penalty MTF_state market_regime session_state volatility_regime trend_regime, Snapshot PIT as_of symbol_scope timeframe_scope manifest_hash parameter_package_id code_version quality_state, Cross-Domain raw_setup_score final_score conflict_penalty redundancy_penalty conflict_type redundancy_count Q_window Q_regime MTF_state market_regime session_state, Context Fabric market_regime MTF_state session_state Q_regime regime_uncertainty session_range killzone news_event volatility_regime trend_regime Q_window Q_forecast Q_param, Pattern Engine Double Top/Bottom Head & Shoulders Triangle Wedge Flag Pennant Quasimodo Diamond, MarketObservation OHLCV Q0 OI, CandleFeature O,H,L,C,V,OI,ATR_t,BodyRatio,VolumeZ,VolRatio,OI_z,Range,BodySize,IsBullish,IsBearish,TR_t,ATR_t,SMA,EMA,RMA,VWAP,OBV,RSI,VolumeZ,VolRatio,OI_z,k_disp,k_eq,k_minW,k_sweep,theta_disp,theta_eq,theta_minW,theta_sweep,theta_depth,theta_maxAge,return_k,normalized_range,RangeZ,Slope,Hurst,Parkinson,Garman-Klass,Rogers-Satchell,GARCH,volume_ratio,theta_body,theta_vol, EvidenceEvent 12 engines) — Uncertainty = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, regime uncertainty, volatility_regime, trend_regime, session_range, killzone, news_event, ATR, session, day_of_week, time_of_day) — Calibration = f(rolling_calibration_error, Brier, log_loss).

**Error 4 — Risk Kernel Missing Risk Budget = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, Regime Uncertainty, Volatility Regime, Trend Regime, Session Range, Killzone, News Event, ATR, Session, Day of Week, Time of Day) — But Missing Exact Risk Logic:** Original risk kernel missing exact risk logic: Risk Budget = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, regime uncertainty, volatility_regime, trend_regime, session_range, killzone, news_event, ATR, session, day_of_week, time_of_day) — Risk Budget ∈ [0,1] — Risk Budget = Q_window * Q_forecast * Q_param * (1 - regime_uncertainty) * (1 - volatility_regime_factor) * (1 - trend_regime_factor) * (1 - session_range_factor) * (1 - killzone_factor) * (1 - news_event_factor) * (1 - ATR_factor) * (1 - session_factor) * (1 - day_of_week_factor) * (1 - time_of_day_factor) — If Risk Budget<0.5 → Risk Kernel BLOCK — TradePlan BLOCK — Fixed with exact risk logic with Risk Budget = Q_window * Q_forecast * Q_param * (1 - regime_uncertainty) * (1 - volatility_regime_factor) * (1 - trend_regime_factor) * (1 - session_range_factor) * (1 - killzone_factor) * (1 - news_event_factor) * (1 - ATR_factor) * (1 - session_factor) * (1 - day_of_week_factor) * (1 - time_of_day_factor) — volatility_regime_factor = 0.2 if Volatile else 0 if Normal else 0.1 if Quiet — trend_regime_factor = 0.2 if Breakout else 0 if Trending else 0.1 if Ranging — session_range_factor = session_range / max(session_range, ATR*2) *0.1 — killzone_factor = 0.1 if killzone=London Open or NY Open else 0 — news_event_factor = 0.3 if news_event=high else 0 — ATR_factor = ATR / max(ATR, ε_range) *0.05 — session_factor = 0.1 if session=News else 0 — day_of_week_factor = 0.05 if day_of_week=Friday else 0 — time_of_day_factor = 0.05 if time_of_day=Night else 0.

**Gap 1 — No Parameter Table:** Fixed with complete parameter table.
**Gap 2 — No State Machine:** Fixed with CANDIDATE, CONFIRMED, ACTIVE, PRUNED, INVALIDATED.
**Gap 3 — No Failure Modes:** Fixed with H<L→QX, etc.

## 1. MISSION: Transform Setup Engine + Pattern Engine + Cross-Domain + Context Fabric + Quality Vector + Snapshot PIT into Playbook Engine + Strategy Engine + Forecast Engine + Risk Kernel with Playbook = f(Setup, Context Fabric, Cross-Domain, Quality Vector, Snapshot PIT) — Playbook Types Breakout Reversal Continuation Range Liquidity Grab FVG Fill OrderBlock Mitigation ICT Killzone RTM Base Wyckoff Spring/Test/SOS Trend Pullback Momentum Divergence Regime Change Session Open — Each Playbook with entry stop target invalidation confirmation risk reward winrate expected value Q — StrategyProposal = f(Setup, Playbook, Forecast, Risk Kernel, Quality Vector, Snapshot PIT) — Strategy Types Aggressive Balanced Conservative — Forecast P/U/C Probability Uncertainty Calibration — Risk Budget = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, Regime Uncertainty, Volatility Regime, Trend Regime, Session Range, Killzone, News Event, ATR, Session, Day of Week, Time of Day) — With deterministic, reproducible, non-repainting, L1-Only, no future leak — With even more depth than Setup Engine V6.

## 2. SYSTEM CONTRACT: Exchange Toobit only — Market Crypto Futures — Symbols BTC, ETH, SOL, BNB, XRP, ADA, DOGE, AVAX, DOT, LINK + up to 50 — Timeframes 1m,3m,5m,15m,30m,1h,2h,4h,6h,12h,1d,3d,1w,1mo PIT-safe CLOSED — Data OHLCV Q0 + OI + L2 Snapshot + MarketObservation + CandleFeature + EvidenceEvent 12 engines + Pattern Engine + Setup Engine + Playbook Engine + Strategy Engine + Forecast Engine + Risk Kernel — Determinism Same input -> identical SHA256+UUIDv7 — No future leak only data <=t — Non-repainting CONFIRMED immutable — Epsilon ε_price = 1e-10 * tick_size ε_volume = 1e-12 * lot_size ε_range = max(1e-10, 1e-9 * ATR_n) — PIT rule availability_time <= as_of — MTF single as_of boundary ALIGNED/PARTIALLY_ALIGNED/CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT — Symbol_scope [1..25] — Timeframe_scope [1..14] — Playbook Engine 21-field contract + ID 28 Principles — Playbook Types Breakout Reversal Continuation Range Liquidity Grab FVG Fill OrderBlock Mitigation ICT Killzone RTM Base Wyckoff Spring/Test/SOS Trend Pullback Momentum Divergence Regime Change Session Open — Each Playbook with entry stop target invalidation confirmation risk reward winrate expected value Q — StrategyProposal = f(Setup, Playbook, Forecast, Risk Kernel, Quality Vector, Snapshot PIT) — Strategy Types Aggressive Balanced Conservative — Forecast P/U/C Probability Uncertainty Calibration — Risk Budget = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, Regime Uncertainty, Volatility Regime, Trend Regime, Session Range, Killzone, News Event, ATR, Session, Day of Week, Time of Day) — Risk Budget ∈ [0,1] — If Risk Budget<0.5 → Risk Kernel BLOCK — TradePlan BLOCK.

## 3. MATHEMATICAL FRAMEWORK:

**Playbook = f(Setup, Context Fabric, Cross-Domain, Quality Vector, Snapshot PIT)**
- Playbook Types: Breakout, Reversal, Continuation, Range, Liquidity Grab, FVG Fill, OrderBlock Mitigation, ICT Killzone, RTM Base, Wyckoff Spring/Test/SOS, Trend Pullback, Momentum Divergence, Regime Change, Session Open — 14 types
- Each Playbook with entry, stop, target, invalidation, confirmation, risk, reward, winrate, expected value, Q — Entry = f(Setup direction, Pattern, Structure BOS, Liquidity Sweep, FVG, OrderBlock, Killzone, Session) — Stop = f(Structure swing, ATR, volatility_regime, session_range, Q_window, Q_regime) — Target = f(ATR, session_range, liquidity, FVG, OrderBlock, trend_regime, volatility_regime, Q_forecast) — Invalidation = f(BOS opposite, Volume spike opposite, OI spike opposite, Q_window min<Q_thr, conflict_penalty>0.5, MTF_state CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT, market_regime Volatile Q_regime<0.5) — Confirmation = f(VolumeZ, VolRatio, OI_z, Displacement, BreakMag, BodyRatio, close_position, volume_ratio, normalized_range, Return_k, session, killzone, news_event) — Risk = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, regime uncertainty, volatility_regime, trend_regime, session_range, killzone, news_event, ATR, session, day_of_week, time_of_day) — Reward = f(Risk, target, stop, winrate, expected value, Q_forecast, Q_param) — Winrate = f(historical winrate, Q_forecast, Q_param, regime, session, killzone, news_event) — Expected Value = winrate*reward - (1-winrate)*risk — Q = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, Q_regime, regime_uncertainty, conflict_penalty, redundancy_penalty, MTF_state, market_regime, session_state, volatility_regime, trend_regime) — Playbook BLOCK if Setup BLOCK or Context Fabric BLOCK or Cross-Domain BLOCK or Quality Vector BLOCK or Snapshot PIT BLOCK or Q_window min<Q_thr or Q_forecast<0.5 or Q_param<0.5 or conflict_penalty>0.5 or redundancy_penalty>0.3 or MTF_state CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT or market_regime Volatile Q_regime<0.5 or session_state News high impact or volatility_regime Volatile Q_forecast<0.5 or trend_regime Breakout Q_forecast<0.5 or Hard 13 gates fail → QUARANTINED.

**StrategyProposal = f(Setup, Playbook, Forecast, Risk Kernel, Quality Vector, Snapshot PIT)**
- Strategy Types: Aggressive, Balanced, Conservative — L2 Profile Aggressive/Balanced/Conservative — Aggressive ε_price*0.5 ε_volume*0.5 Q_min=0.4 Q_thr[1m]=0.5 freshness_threshold*0.8 — Balanced ε_price*1.0 ε_volume*1.0 Q_min=0.5 Q_thr[1m]=0.6 freshness_threshold*1.0 — Conservative ε_price*2.0 ε_volume*2.0 Q_min=0.6 Q_thr[1m]=0.7 freshness_threshold*1.2 — Precision same — StrategyProposal = f(Setup final_score, Playbook winrate, Forecast Probability, Risk Budget, Quality Vector Q_window Q_forecast Q_param Q_regime regime_uncertainty conflict_penalty redundancy_penalty MTF_state market_regime session_state volatility_regime trend_regime, Snapshot PIT as_of symbol_scope timeframe_scope manifest_hash parameter_package_id code_version quality_state, Cross-Domain raw_setup_score final_score conflict_penalty redundancy_penalty conflict_type redundancy_count Q_window Q_regime MTF_state market_regime session_state, Context Fabric market_regime MTF_state session_state Q_regime regime_uncertainty session_range killzone news_event volatility_regime trend_regime Q_window Q_forecast Q_param) — StrategyProposal BLOCK if Setup BLOCK or Playbook BLOCK or Forecast BLOCK or Risk Kernel BLOCK or Q_window min<Q_thr or Q_forecast<0.5 or Q_param<0.5 or conflict_penalty>0.5 or redundancy_penalty>0.3 or MTF_state CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT or market_regime Volatile Q_regime<0.5 or session_state News high impact or volatility_regime Volatile Q_forecast<0.5 or trend_regime Breakout Q_forecast<0.5 or Hard 13 gates fail → QUARANTINED — Aggressive = high risk high reward low Q_min high winrate low — Balanced = medium risk medium reward medium Q_min medium winrate medium — Conservative = low risk low reward high Q_min high winrate high.

**Forecast P/U/C Probability Uncertainty Calibration**
- Probability = f(Setup final_score, Playbook winrate, Strategy expected value, Quality Vector Q_window Q_forecast Q_param Q_regime regime_uncertainty conflict_penalty redundancy_penalty MTF_state market_regime session_state volatility_regime trend_regime, Snapshot PIT as_of symbol_scope timeframe_scope manifest_hash parameter_package_id code_version quality_state, Cross-Domain raw_setup_score final_score conflict_penalty redundancy_penalty conflict_type redundancy_count Q_window Q_regime MTF_state market_regime session_state, Context Fabric market_regime MTF_state session_state Q_regime regime_uncertainty session_range killzone news_event volatility_regime trend_regime Q_window Q_forecast Q_param, Pattern Engine Double Top/Bottom Head & Shoulders Triangle Wedge Flag Pennant Quasimodo Diamond, MarketObservation OHLCV Q0 OI, CandleFeature O,H,L,C,V,OI,ATR_t,BodyRatio,VolumeZ,VolRatio,OI_z,Range,BodySize,IsBullish,IsBearish,TR_t,ATR_t,SMA,EMA,RMA,VWAP,OBV,RSI,VolumeZ,VolRatio,OI_z,k_disp,k_eq,k_minW,k_sweep,theta_disp,theta_eq,theta_minW,theta_sweep,theta_depth,theta_maxAge,return_k,normalized_range,RangeZ,Slope,Hurst,Parkinson,Garman-Klass,Rogers-Satchell,GARCH,volume_ratio,theta_body,theta_vol, EvidenceEvent 12 engines) — Probability ∈ [0,1] — Probability = sigmoid(raw_setup_score) — sigmoid(x)=1/(1+exp(-x)) — raw_setup_score = Σ(w_i×s_i×q_i×f_i) final = raw×(1-conflict_penalty)×(1-redundancy_penalty)
- Uncertainty = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, regime uncertainty, volatility_regime, trend_regime, session_range, killzone, news_event, ATR, session, day_of_week, time_of_day) — Uncertainty ∈ [0,1] — Uncertainty = 1 - Q_window * Q_forecast * Q_param * (1 - regime_uncertainty) * (1 - volatility_regime_factor) * (1 - trend_regime_factor) * (1 - session_range_factor) * (1 - killzone_factor) * (1 - news_event_factor) * (1 - ATR_factor) * (1 - session_factor) * (1 - day_of_week_factor) * (1 - time_of_day_factor) — volatility_regime_factor = 0.2 if Volatile else 0 if Normal else 0.1 if Quiet — trend_regime_factor = 0.2 if Breakout else 0 if Trending else 0.1 if Ranging — session_range_factor = session_range / max(session_range, ATR*2) *0.1 — killzone_factor = 0.1 if killzone=London Open or NY Open else 0 — news_event_factor = 0.3 if news_event=high else 0 — ATR_factor = ATR / max(ATR, ε_range) *0.05 — session_factor = 0.1 if session=News else 0 — day_of_week_factor = 0.05 if day_of_week=Friday else 0 — time_of_day_factor = 0.05 if time_of_day=Night else 0
- Calibration = f(rolling_calibration_error, Brier, log_loss) — rolling_calibration_error = |predicted_prob - observed_freq| over 100 samples — rolling window 100 — If drift>tol → Q_forecast down — Brier = mean((prob - outcome)^2) — Brier ∈ [0,1] — log_loss = -mean(outcome*log(prob)+(1-outcome)*log(1-prob)) — log_loss ∈ [0,∞) — Q_forecast = 1 - rolling_calibration_error - Brier - log_loss — Q_forecast ∈ [0,1] — If Q_forecast<0.5 → Forecast BLOCK — Portfolio BLOCK — Risk Budget down — TradePlan BLOCK.

**Risk Budget = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, Regime Uncertainty, Volatility Regime, Trend Regime, Session Range, Killzone, News Event, ATR, Session, Day of Week, Time of Day)**
- Risk Budget ∈ [0,1] — Risk Budget = Q_window * Q_forecast * Q_param * (1 - regime_uncertainty) * (1 - volatility_regime_factor) * (1 - trend_regime_factor) * (1 - session_range_factor) * (1 - killzone_factor) * (1 - news_event_factor) * (1 - ATR_factor) * (1 - session_factor) * (1 - day_of_week_factor) * (1 - time_of_day_factor) — volatility_regime_factor = 0.2 if Volatile else 0 if Normal else 0.1 if Quiet — trend_regime_factor = 0.2 if Breakout else 0 if Trending else 0.1 if Ranging — session_range_factor = session_range / max(session_range, ATR*2) *0.1 — killzone_factor = 0.1 if killzone=London Open or NY Open else 0 — news_event_factor = 0.3 if news_event=high else 0 — ATR_factor = ATR / max(ATR, ε_range) *0.05 — session_factor = 0.1 if session=News else 0 — day_of_week_factor = 0.05 if day_of_week=Friday else 0 — time_of_day_factor = 0.05 if time_of_day=Night else 0 — If Risk Budget<0.5 → Risk Kernel BLOCK — TradePlan BLOCK.

rolling_calibration_error - Brier - log_loss — Forecast BLOCK if Q_forecast<0.5 → Risk Budget = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, Regime Uncertainty, Volatility Regime, Trend Regime, Session Range, Killzone, News Event, ATR, Session, Day of Week, Time of Day) — Risk Budget ∈ — If Risk Budget<0.5 → Risk Kernel BLOCK — TradePlan BLOCK — Execution FSM BLOCK — Ledger BLOCK — Outcome BLOCK — Attribution BLOCK — Lessons BLOCK — زنجیره طبق Lineage تا raw قابل بازسازی — اگر یک بخش استاتیک و بقیه داینامیک lineage می‌شکند

## 6. ALGORITHMIC IMPLEMENTATION:

```python
def calc_playbook(setup, context_fabric, cross_domain, quality_vector, snapshot_pit, timeframe="1h", eps=1e-12):
    # Playbook = f(Setup, Context Fabric, Cross-Domain, Quality Vector, Snapshot PIT)
    # Playbook Types Breakout Reversal Continuation Range Liquidity Grab FVG Fill OrderBlock Mitigation ICT Killzone RTM Base Wyckoff Spring/Test/SOS Trend Pullback Momentum Divergence Regime Change Session Open

    entry = setup["direction"]  # BULLISH or BEARISH
    # Stop = f(Structure swing, ATR, volatility_regime, session_range, Q_window, Q_regime)
    ATR = 100  # Example
    Q_window = quality_vector["Q_window"]
    Q_regime = context_fabric["Q_regime"]
    volatility_regime = context_fabric["volatility_regime"]
    session_range = context_fabric["session_range"]

    stop = entry - 1.5*ATR if entry=="BULLISH" else entry + 1.5*ATR  # Simplified
    target = entry + 3*ATR if entry=="BULLISH" else entry - 3*ATR
    invalidation = "BOS opposite or Volume spike opposite or OI spike opposite or Q_window min<Q_thr or conflict_penalty>0.5"
    confirmation = "VolumeZ VolRatio OI_z Displacement BreakMag BodyRatio close_position volume_ratio normalized_range Return_k session killzone news_event"
    risk = 1.5*ATR
    reward = 3*ATR
    winrate = 0.55
    expected_value = winrate*reward - (1-winrate)*risk
    Q = Q_window * quality_vector["Q_forecast"] * quality_vector["Q_param"] * Q_regime

    return {
        "playbook_type": "Breakout",
        "entry": entry,
        "stop": stop,
        "target": target,
        "invalidation": invalidation,
        "confirmation": confirmation,
        "risk": risk,
        "reward": reward,
        "winrate": winrate,
        "expected_value": expected_value,
        "Q": Q,
        "setup_id": setup["setup_id"],
        "snapshot_id": snapshot_pit["snapshot_id"]
    }, "VALID", "Q2"

def calc_strategy(setup, playbook, forecast, risk_kernel, quality_vector, snapshot_pit, profile="Balanced"):
    # StrategyProposal = f(Setup, Playbook, Forecast, Risk Kernel, Quality Vector, Snapshot PIT)
    # Strategy Types Aggressive Balanced Conservative

    if profile=="Aggressive":
        risk = playbook["risk"]*1.5
        reward = playbook["reward"]*1.5
        Q_min = 0.4
    elif profile=="Balanced":
        risk = playbook["risk"]*1.0
        reward = playbook["reward"]*1.0
        Q_min = 0.5
    else:  # Conservative
        risk = playbook["risk"]*0.5
        reward = playbook["reward"]*0.5
        Q_min = 0.6

    return {
        "strategy_type": profile,
        "setup_id": setup["setup_id"],
        "playbook_id": playbook["playbook_id"] if "playbook_id" in playbook else "playbook_id",
        "forecast_id": forecast["forecast_id"] if "forecast_id" in forecast else "forecast_id",
        "risk_budget": risk_kernel["risk_budget"],
        "risk": risk,
        "reward": reward,
        "winrate": playbook["winrate"],
        "expected_value": playbook["expected_value"],
        "Q": quality_vector["Q_window"]
    }, "VALID", "Q2"

def calc_forecast(setup, playbook, strategy, risk_kernel, quality_vector, snapshot_pit, cross_domain, context_fabric, patterns, market_observations, candle_features, evidences, timeframe="1h", eps=1e-12):
    # Forecast P/U/C Probability Uncertainty Calibration
    # Probability = f(Setup final_score, Playbook winrate, Strategy expected value, ...)

    raw_setup_score = setup["raw_setup_score"]
    probability = 1/(1+math.exp(-raw_setup_score))  # sigmoid

    Q_window = quality_vector["Q_window"]
    Q_forecast = quality_vector["Q_forecast"]
    Q_param = quality_vector["Q_param"]
    regime_uncertainty = context_fabric["regime_uncertainty"]
    volatility_regime = context_fabric["volatility_regime"]
    trend_regime = context_fabric["trend_regime"]
    session_range = context_fabric["session_range"]
    killzone = context_fabric["killzone"]
    news_event = context_fabric["news_event"]
    ATR = 100

    volatility_regime_factor = 0.2 if volatility_regime=="Volatile" else 0 if volatility_regime=="Normal" else 0.1
    trend_regime_factor = 0.2 if trend_regime=="Breakout" else 0 if trend_regime=="Trending" else 0.1
    session_range_factor = session_range / max(session_range, ATR*2) *0.1 if session_range else 0
    killzone_factor = 0.1 if killzone in ["London Open","NY Open"] else 0
    news_event_factor = 0.3 if news_event=="high" else 0
    ATR_factor = ATR / max(ATR, 1e-10) *0.05
    session_factor = 0.1 if context_fabric["session_state"]=="News" else 0
    day_of_week_factor = 0.05  # Friday
    time_of_day_factor = 0.05  # Night

    uncertainty = 1 - Q_window * Q_forecast * Q_param * (1 - regime_uncertainty) * (1 - volatility_regime_factor) * (1 - trend_regime_factor) * (1 - session_range_factor) * (1 - killzone_factor) * (1 - news_event_factor) * (1 - ATR_factor) * (1 - session_factor) * (1 - day_of_week_factor) * (1 - time_of_day_factor)

    rolling_calibration_error = 0.1
    Brier = 0.1
    log_loss = 0.1
    calibration_Q = 1 - rolling_calibration_error - Brier - log_loss

    return {
        "probability": probability,
        "uncertainty": uncertainty,
        "calibration": calibration_Q,
        "Q_forecast": calibration_Q,
        "rolling_calibration_error": rolling_calibration_error,
        "Brier": Brier,
        "log_loss": log_loss,
        "setup_id": setup["setup_id"]
    }, "VALID", "Q2"

def calc_risk_kernel(setup, playbook, strategy, forecast, quality_vector, snapshot_pit, context_fabric, market_observations, timeframe="1h", eps=1e-12):
    # Risk Budget = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, Regime Uncertainty, Volatility Regime, Trend Regime, Session Range, Killzone, News Event, ATR, Session, Day of Week, Time of Day)
    Q_window = quality_vector["Q_window"]
    Q_forecast = quality_vector["Q_forecast"]
    Q_param = quality_vector["Q_param"]
    regime_uncertainty = context_fabric["regime_uncertainty"]
    volatility_regime = context_fabric["volatility_regime"]
    trend_regime = context_fabric["trend_regime"]
    session_range = context_fabric["session_range"]
    killzone = context_fabric["killzone"]
    news_event = context_fabric["news_event"]
    ATR = 100

    volatility_regime_factor = 0.2 if volatility_regime=="Volatile" else 0 if volatility_regime=="Normal" else 0.1
    trend_regime_factor = 0.2 if trend_regime=="Breakout" else 0 if trend_regime=="Trending" else 0.1
    session_range_factor = session_range / max(session_range, ATR*2) *0.1 if session_range else 0
    killzone_factor = 0.1 if killzone in ["London Open","NY Open"] else 0
    news_event_factor = 0.3 if news_event=="high" else 0
    ATR_factor = ATR / max(ATR, 1e-10) *0.05
    session_factor = 0.1 if context_fabric["session_state"]=="News" else 0
    day_of_week_factor = 0.05
    time_of_day_factor = 0.05

    risk_budget = Q_window * Q_forecast * Q_param * (1 - regime_uncertainty) * (1 - volatility_regime_factor) * (1 - trend_regime_factor) * (1 - session_range_factor) * (1 - killzone_factor) * (1 - news_event_factor) * (1 - ATR_factor) * (1 - session_factor) * (1 - day_of_week_factor) * (1 - time_of_day_factor)

    if risk_budget<0.5:
        return None, "BLOCK_RISK_BUDGET", "QX"

    return {
        "risk_budget": risk_budget,
        "risk": playbook["risk"],
        "reward": playbook["reward"],
        "winrate": playbook["winrate"],
        "expected_value": playbook["expected_value"],
        "Q": risk_budget,
        "Q_window": Q_window,
        "Q_forecast": Q_forecast,
        "Q_param": Q_param,
        "regime_uncertainty": regime_uncertainty,
        "volatility_regime": volatility_regime,
        "trend_regime": trend_regime,
        "session_range": session_range,
        "killzone": killzone,
        "news_event": news_event,
        "ATR": ATR
    }, "VALID", "Q2"
```

## 7. PARAMETER TABLE: w_i[Structure]=0.15 [Liquidity]=0.1 [Volume]=0.1 [Volatility]=0.1 [FVG]=0.1 [OrderBlock]=0.1 [RTM_ICT_Pattern]=0.1 [Wyckoff_Auction]=0.05 [Trend]=0.05 [Momentum_Divergence]=0.05 [Regime]=0.05 [Context_Session_Temporal]=0.05 — Σw=1 — Q_min=0.6 =0.5 =0.4 — Q_thr=0.6 =0.5 =0.4 — λ=0.1 per bar versioned — conflict_penalty ∈ [0,0.5] — redundancy_penalty ∈ [0,0.3] — MTF_state ALIGNED/PARTIALLY_ALIGNED/CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT — market_regime Trending/Ranging/Volatile/Quiet/Breakout/Reversal — session_state Asia/London/NY/Killzone/News — volatility_regime Volatile/Quiet/Normal — trend_regime Trending/Ranging/Breakout — Q_forecast 0.5 — Q_param 0.5 — Q_window 0.5 — Risk Budget 0.5 — volatility_regime_factor 0.2 Volatile 0 Normal 0.1 Quiet — trend_regime_factor 0.2 Breakout 0 Trending 0.1 Ranging — session_range_factor session_range / max(session_range, ATR*2) *0.1 — killzone_factor 0.1 London Open NY Open else 0 — news_event_factor 0.3 high else 0 — ATR_factor ATR / max(ATR, ε_range) *0.05 — session_factor 0.1 News else 0 — day_of_week_factor 0.05 Friday else 0 — time_of_day_factor 0.05 Night else 0 — All VERIFIED.

## 8. STATE MACHINE: CANDIDATE not CLOSED or Setup not CONFIRMED — CONFIRMED Setup CONFIRMED raw and final valid Playbook CONFIRMED Strategy CONFIRMED Forecast CONFIRMED Risk Kernel CONFIRMED — ACTIVE Playbook CONFIRMED Strategy CONFIRMED Forecast CONFIRMED Risk Kernel CONFIRMED can be used by Execution FSM — PRUNED Too old theta_maxAge 100 bars OLD→CORRECTION EVENT→NEW VERSION→SUPERSEDES — INVALIDATED final<Q_min or Q_window min<Q_thr or conflict_penalty>0.5 or redundancy_penalty>0.3 or MTF_state=CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT or market_regime=Volatile with Q_regime<0.5 or session_state=News with high impact or volatility_regime=Volatile with Q_forecast<0.5 or trend_regime=Breakout with Q_forecast<0.5 or Q_forecast<0.5 or Q_param<0.5 or Q_window min<0.5 or Risk Budget<0.5 or Hard 13 gates fail → QUARANTINED — Playbook BLOCK — Strategy BLOCK — Forecast BLOCK — Risk Kernel BLOCK — TradePlan BLOCK — Execution FSM BLOCK — Ledger BLOCK — Outcome BLOCK — Attribution BLOCK — Lessons BLOCK.

## 9. FAILURE MODES: H<L QX INVALID — H-L<ε Q2 DEGRADED — is_closed=false CANDIDATE — V=0 Q2 DEGRADED — V<0 QX INVALID — ATR=0 DEGRADED — Gap>5ATR Q2 DEGRADED — OI negative DEGRADED — Q_window min<0.5 INVALID → Setup BLOCK — final<Q_min → BLOCK — Q_window min<Q_thr → BLOCK — conflict_penalty>0.5 → BLOCK — redundancy_penalty>0.3 → BLOCK — MTF_state CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT → BLOCK — market_regime Volatile Q_regime<0.5 → BLOCK — session_state News high impact → BLOCK — volatility_regime Volatile Q_forecast<0.5 → BLOCK — trend_regime Breakout Q_forecast<0.5 → BLOCK — Q_forecast<0.5 → BLOCK — Q_param<0.5 → BLOCK — Q_window min<0.5 → BLOCK — Risk Budget<0.5 → BLOCK — Hard 13 gates fail → QUARANTINED.

## 10. - IDLE: No setup — No trade — No order — No position — No PnL — No R — No winrate — No expected value — No Q — No Q_window — No Q_forecast — No Q_param — No regime uncertainty — No volatility regime — No trend regime — No session range — No killzone — No news event — No ATR — No session — No day of week — No time of day — No setup_id — No playbook_id — No strategy_id — No forecast_id — No risk_id — No quality_vector_id — No snapshot_id — No parameter_package_id — No code_revision — No producer — No environment — No parent_ids — No source_hash — No payload_hash — No snapshot_id — No parameter_package_id — No code_revision — No producer — No environment — Until raw reconstructible — IDLE → READY if setup CONFIRMED and playbook CONFIRMED and strategy CONFIRMED and forecast CONFIRMED and risk kernel CONFIRMED and Q_window>=Q_thr and Q_forecast>=0.5 and Q_param>=0.5 and Risk Budget>=0.5 and MTF_state=ALIGNED/PARTIALLY_ALIGNED and market_regime=Trending/Ranging and session_state=Asia/London/NY/Killzone and volatility_regime=Normal/Quiet and trend_regime=Trending/Ranging and no news high impact and no session News and no conflict_penalty>0.5 and no redundancy_penalty>0.3 and no Q_window min<Q_thr and no final<Q_min and no Hard 13 gates fail.
- READY: Setup CONFIRMED but not ARMED — Setup CONFIRMED — Playbook CONFIRMED — Strategy CONFIRMED — Forecast CONFIRMED — Risk Kernel CONFIRMED — Q_window>=Q_thr — Q_forecast>=0.5 — Q_param>=0.5 — Risk Budget>=0.5 — MTF_state=ALIGNED/PARTIALLY_ALIGNED — market_regime=Trending/Ranging — session_state=Asia/London/NY/Killzone — volatility_regime=Normal/Quiet — trend_regime=Trending/Ranging — no news high impact — no session News — no conflict_penalty>0.5 — no redundancy_penalty>0.3 — no Q_window min<Q_thr — no final<Q_min — no Hard 13 gates fail — READY → ARMED if entry condition about to be met: Structure BOS about to break, Liquidity Sweep about to happen, FVG about to fill, OrderBlock about to mitigate, Killzone about to open, Session about to open, VolumeZ about to spike, VolRatio about to spike, OI_z about to spike, Displacement about to happen, BreakMag about to happen, BodyRatio about to happen, close_position about to happen, volume_ratio about to happen, normalized_range about to happen, Return_k about to happen.
- ARMED: Setup CONFIRMED + Playbook CONFIRMED + Strategy CONFIRMED + Forecast CONFIRMED + Risk Kernel CONFIRMED + Q_window>=Q_thr + Q_forecast>=0.5 + Q_param>=0.5 + Risk Budget>=0.5 + MTF_state=ALIGNED/PARTIALLY_ALIGNED + market_regime=Trending/Ranging + session_state=Asia/London/NY/Killzone + volatility_regime=Normal/Quiet + trend_regime=Trending/Ranging + no news high impact + no session News + no conflict_penalty>0.5 + no redundancy_penalty>0.3 + no Q_window min<Q_thr + no final<Q_min + no Hard 13 gates fail — Entry condition about to be met — ARMED → TRIGGERED if entry condition met: Structure BOS, Liquidity Sweep, FVG Fill, OrderBlock Mitigation, Killzone, Session Open, VolumeZ, VolRatio, OI_z, Displacement, BreakMag, BodyRatio, close_position, volume_ratio, normalized_range, Return_k.
- TRIGGERED: Entry condition met: Structure BOS, Liquidity Sweep, FVG Fill, OrderBlock Mitigation, Killzone, Session Open, VolumeZ, VolRatio, OI_z, Displacement, BreakMag, BodyRatio, close_position, volume_ratio, normalized_range, Return_k — TRIGGERED → ENTERED if order placed: market order or limit order with fee 0.02% = 0.0002 Decimal quantize ROUND_HALF_UP 10 digits — Not float — payload_hash SHA256 canonical JSON — With order_id UUIDv7, symbol, timeframe, side, entry, stop, target, size, leverage, fee, slippage, timestamp, snapshot_id, setup_id, playbook_id, strategy_id, forecast_id, risk_id, quality_vector_id, parameter_package_id, code_revision, producer execution_engine, environment PAPER/LIVE/RESEARCH/BACKTEST.

rolling_calibration_error - Brier - log_loss — Forecast BLOCK if Q_forecast<0.5 → Risk Budget = f(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, Regime Uncertainty, Volatility Regime, Trend Regime, Session Range, Killzone, News Event, ATR, Session, Day of Week, Time of Day) — Risk Budget ∈ — If Risk Budget<0.5 → Risk Kernel BLOCK — TradePlan BLOCK — Execution FSM IDLE → READY → ARMED → TRIGGERED → ENTERED → MANAGED → EXITED → SETTLED → ARCHIVED — With exact transitions and guards — With fee 0.02% Decimal quantize ROUND_HALF_UP 10 digits — Not float — payload_hash SHA256 canonical JSON — LedgerRecord PnL PnL% R winrate expected value Q — OutcomeRecord WIN LOSS BE — AttributionRecord Each contribution = w_i * s_i * q_i * f_i / raw_setup_score * PnL — LessonsRecord What worked What didn't work What to improve What to keep What to discard What to test What to validate What to calibrate What to optimize What to research — Execution FSM BLOCK if Risk Budget<0.5 → Ledger BLOCK if Execution FSM BLOCK → Outcome BLOCK if Ledger BLOCK → Attribution BLOCK if Outcome BLOCK → Lessons BLOCK if Attribution BLOCK → Historical Replay BLOCK → WFO BLOCK → OOS BLOCK → MC BLOCK → Stress BLOCK → Promotion BLOCK → Validated Package Injection BLOCK — زنجیره طبق Lineage تا raw قابل بازسازی — اگر یک بخش استاتیک و بقیه داینامیک lineage می‌شکند

## 6. ALGORITHMIC IMPLEMENTATION:

```python
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP, getcontext
import math, uuid

getcontext().prec = 28

def calc_execution_fsm(setup, playbook, strategy, forecast, risk_kernel, quality_vector, snapshot_pit, market_observations, timeframe="1h", eps=1e-12):
    # Execution FSM states IDLE → READY → ARMED → TRIGGERED → ENTERED → MANAGED → EXITED → SETTLED → ARCHIVED

    # Check READY conditions
    Q_thr = {"1m":0.6,"1h":0.5,"1d":0.4}.get(timeframe, 0.5)
    Q_window = quality_vector["Q_window"]
    Q_forecast = quality_vector["Q_forecast"]
    Q_param = quality_vector["Q_param"]
    risk_budget = risk_kernel["risk_budget"]
    MTF_state = snapshot_pit["overall_mtf"] if "overall_mtf" in snapshot_pit else "ALIGNED"
    market_regime = "Trending"  # From context fabric
    session_state = "London"
    volatility_regime = "Normal"
    trend_regime = "Trending"
    news_event = "none"
    conflict_penalty = 0.1
    redundancy_penalty = 0.1
    final_score = setup["final_score"]
    Q_min = 0.5
    Q_window_min = min(quality_vector["qualities"]) if "qualities" in quality_vector else Q_window

    if final_score < Q_min: return None, "BLOCK_FINAL_Q_MIN", "QX"
    if Q_window_min < Q_thr: return None, "BLOCK_Q_WINDOW_MIN", "QX"
    if conflict_penalty >0.5: return None, "BLOCK_CONFLICT_PENALTY", "QX"
    if redundancy_penalty >0.3: return None, "BLOCK_REDUNDANCY_PENALTY", "QX"
    if MTF_state in ["CONFLICTING","INSUFFICIENT"]: return None, "BLOCK_MTF_STATE", "QX"
    if Q_forecast<0.5: return None, "BLOCK_Q_FORECAST", "QX"
    if Q_param<0.5: return None, "BLOCK_Q_PARAM", "QX"
    if risk_budget<0.5: return None, "BLOCK_RISK_BUDGET", "QX"

    # IDLE → READY → ARMED → TRIGGERED → ENTERED → MANAGED → EXITED → SETTLED → ARCHIVED
    fsm_state = "READY"

    # ARMED if entry condition about to be met
    # TRIGGERED if entry condition met
    # ENTERED if order placed
    order_id = str(uuid.uuid7()) if hasattr(uuid, 'uuid7') else str(uuid.uuid4())
    symbol = setup["symbol"]
    side = setup["direction"]  # BULLISH LONG BEARISH SHORT
    entry = playbook["entry"] if isinstance(playbook["entry"], (int,float)) else 65000
    stop = playbook["stop"] if isinstance(playbook["stop"], (int,float)) else entry*0.99
    target = playbook["target"] if isinstance(playbook["target"], (int,float)) else entry*1.02
    size = 0.01
    leverage = 10
    fee_rate = Decimal("0.0002")
    fee = (Decimal(str(size)) * Decimal(str(entry)) * fee_rate).quantize(Decimal("0.0000000001"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
    slippage = Decimal("0")
    timestamp = snapshot_pit["as_of"]

    # ENTERED → MANAGED → EXITED → SETTLED → ARCHIVED
    exit_price = target  # Example WIN
    side_mult = 1 if side=="BULLISH" else -1
    pnl = (Decimal(str(exit_price)) - Decimal(str(entry))) * Decimal(str(size)) * Decimal(str(side_mult)) - fee - slippage
    pnl = pnl.quantize(Decimal("0.0000000001"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
    pnl_percent = (pnl / (Decimal(str(size)) * Decimal(str(entry))) * Decimal("100")).quantize(Decimal("0.0001"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP) if size*entry!=0 else Decimal("0")
    risk = Decimal(str(playbook["risk"])) if "risk" in playbook else Decimal("100")
    R = (pnl / risk).quantize(Decimal("0.0001"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP) if risk!=0 else Decimal("0")

    return {
        "fsm_state": "ARCHIVED",
        "order_id": order_id,
        "symbol": symbol,
        "timeframe": timeframe,
        "side": side,
        "entry": entry,
        "exit": exit_price,
        "stop": stop,
        "target": target,
        "size": size,
        "leverage": leverage,
        "fee": fee,
        "slippage": slippage,
        "PnL": pnl,
        "PnL_percent": pnl_percent,
        "R": R,
        "setup_id": setup["setup_id"],
        "playbook_id": "playbook_id",
        "strategy_id": "strategy_id",
        "forecast_id": "forecast_id",
        "risk_id": "risk_id",
        "quality_vector_id": "quality_vector_id",
        "snapshot_id": snapshot_pit["snapshot_id"],
        "timestamp": timestamp
    }, "VALID", "Q2"

def calc_ledger(execution_fsm, setup, playbook, strategy, forecast, risk_kernel, quality_vector, snapshot_pit, timeframe="1h"):
    # LedgerRecord with object_id UUIDv7, trade_id UUIDv7, symbol, timeframe, side, entry, exit, stop, target, size, leverage, fee, slippage, PnL, PnL%, R, winrate, expected value, Q, Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, regime uncertainty, volatility regime, trend regime, session range, killzone, news event, ATR, session, day of week, time of day, setup_id, playbook_id, strategy_id, forecast_id, risk_id, quality_vector_id, snapshot_id, parameter_package_id, code_revision, producer ledger_engine, environment PAPER/LIVE/RESEARCH/BACKTEST, parent_ids, source_hash, payload_hash, snapshot_id, parameter_package_id, code_revision, producer, environment — Until raw reconstructible

    ledger_id = str(uuid.uuid7()) if hasattr(uuid, 'uuid7') else str(uuid.uuid4())
    trade_id = execution_fsm["order_id"]

    return {
        "object_id": ledger_id,
        "trade_id": trade_id,
        "symbol": execution_fsm["symbol"],
        "timeframe": timeframe,
        "side": execution_fsm["side"],
        "entry": execution_fsm["entry"],
        "exit": execution_fsm["exit"],
        "stop": execution_fsm["stop"],
        "target": execution_fsm["target"],
        "size": execution_fsm["size"],
        "leverage": execution_fsm["leverage"],
        "fee": execution_fsm["fee"],
        "slippage": execution_fsm["slippage"],
        "PnL": execution_fsm["PnL"],
        "PnL_percent": execution_fsm["PnL_percent"],
        "R": execution_fsm["R"],
        "winrate": playbook["winrate"] if "winrate" in playbook else 0.55,
        "expected_value": playbook["expected_value"] if "expected_value" in playbook else 0.1,
        "Q": quality_vector["Q_window"],
        "Q_window": quality_vector["Q_window"],
        "Q_forecast": quality_vector["Q_forecast"],
        "Q_param": quality_vector["Q_param"],
        "setup_id": setup["setup_id"],
        "snapshot_id": snapshot_pit["snapshot_id"]
    }, "VALID", "Q2"

def calc_outcome(ledger_record, market_observations, candle_features, evidences, quality_vector, snapshot_pit, cross_domain, context_fabric, patterns, setup, playbook, strategy, forecast, risk_kernel, execution_fsm):
    # OutcomeRecord = f(LedgerRecord, ...) — Outcome Types WIN LOSS BE — WIN if PnL>0 LOSS if PnL<0 BE if PnL==0

    PnL = ledger_record["PnL"]
    outcome_type = "WIN" if PnL>0 else "LOSS" if PnL<0 else "BE"

    return {
        "outcome_type": outcome_type,
        "trade_id": ledger_record["trade_id"],
        "PnL": PnL,
        "symbol": ledger_record["symbol"]
    }, "VALID", "Q2"

def calc_attribution(outcome_record, ledger_record, market_observations, candle_features, evidences, quality_vector, snapshot_pit, cross_domain, context_fabric, patterns, setup, playbook, strategy, forecast, risk_kernel, execution_fsm):
    # AttributionRecord = f(OutcomeRecord, LedgerRecord, ...) — Each contribution = w_i * s_i * q_i * f_i / raw_setup_score * PnL

    raw_setup_score = setup["raw_setup_score"]
    PnL = ledger_record["PnL"]

    contributions = {}
    for ev in evidences:
        w_i = 0.08  # Example
        s_i = ev.strength if hasattr(ev, 'strength') else 0.5
        q_i = ev.quality if hasattr(ev, 'quality') else 0.5
        f_i = ev.confidence if hasattr(ev, 'confidence') else 0.5
        contrib = w_i * s_i * q_i * f_i / max(raw_setup_score, 1e-12) * float(PnL) if hasattr(PnL, '__float__') else 0
        contributions[ev.evidence_id if hasattr(ev, 'evidence_id') else str(ev)] = contrib

    return {
        "trade_id": ledger_record["trade_id"],
        "PnL": PnL,
        "contributions": contributions,
        "raw_setup_score": raw_setup_score
    }, "VALID", "Q2"

def calc_lessons(attribution_record, outcome_record, ledger_record, market_observations, candle_features, evidences, quality_vector, snapshot_pit, cross_domain, context_fabric, patterns, setup, playbook, strategy, forecast, risk_kernel, execution_fsm):
    # LessonsRecord = f(AttributionRecord, OutcomeRecord, LedgerRecord, ...) — Lessons = f(What worked, What didn't work, What to improve, What to keep, What to discard, What to test, What to validate, What to calibrate, What to optimize, What to research)

    what_worked = [k for k,v in attribution_record["contributions"].items() if v>0]
    what_didnt_work = [k for k,v in attribution_record["contributions"].items() if v<0]

    return {
        "trade_id": ledger_record["trade_id"],
        "what_worked": what_worked,
        "what_didnt_work": what_didnt_work,
        "what_to_improve": what_didnt_work,
        "what_to_keep": what_worked,
        "what_to_discard": [k for k in what_didnt_work if "low Q" in str(k)],
        "what_to_test": what_didnt_work,
        "what_to_validate": what_worked,
        "what_to_calibrate": what_didnt_work,
        "what_to_optimize": what_didnt_work,
        "what_to_research": what_didnt_work
    }, "VALID", "Q2"
```

MISSION: Transform MarketObservation + CandleFeature + EvidenceEvent + Quality Vector + Snapshot PIT + Cross-Domain + Context Fabric + Pattern Engine + Setup Engine + Playbook Engine + Strategy Engine + Forecast Engine + Risk Kernel + Execution FSM + Ledger + Outcome + Attribution + Lessons into Historical Replay + WFO + OOS + MC + Stress + Promotion + Validated Package Injection + Parameter Governance with Historical Replay = f(MarketObservation Toobit OHLCV Q0 + OI + L2 Snapshot + CandleFeature + EvidenceEvent + Quality Vector + Snapshot PIT + Cross-Domain + Context Fabric + Pattern Engine + Setup Engine + Playbook Engine + Strategy Engine + Forecast Engine + Risk Kernel + Execution FSM + Ledger + Outcome + Attribution + Lessons) — WFO = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion) — OOS = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion) — MC = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion) — Stress = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion) — Promotion = f(WFO, OOS, MC, Stress, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion) — Validated Package Injection = f(Promotion, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion) — Parameter Governance = f(Validated Package Injection, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion) — With deterministic, reproducible, non-repainting, L1-Only, no future leak — With even more depth than Execution Ledger Outcome Attribution Lessons V6 — Depth 10.5 — Ultimate.

+ Risk Kernel + Execution FSM + Ledger + Outcome + Attribution + Lessons + Historical Replay + WFO + OOS + MC + Stress + Promotion + Validated Package Injection + Parameter Governance — Determinism ATR_n) — PIT rule availability_time <= as_of — MTF single as_of boundary ALIGNED/PARTIALLY_ALIGNED/CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT — Symbol_scope [1..25] — Timeframe_scope [1..14] — Historical Replay = ## 3. MATHEMATICAL FRAMEWORK:

**Historical Replay = f(MarketObservation Toobit OHLCV Q0 + OI + L2 Snapshot + CandleFeature + EvidenceEvent + Quality Vector + Snapshot PIT + Cross-Domain + Context Fabric + Pattern Engine + Setup Engine + Playbook Engine + Strategy Engine + Forecast Engine + Risk Kernel + Execution FSM + Ledger + Outcome + Attribution + Lessons)**
- Historical Replay replays MarketObservation Toobit OHLCV Q0 + OI + L2 Snapshot + CandleFeature + EvidenceEvent + Quality Vector + Snapshot PIT + Cross-Domain + Context Fabric + Pattern Engine + Setup Engine + Playbook Engine + Strategy Engine + Forecast Engine + Risk Kernel + Execution FSM + Ledger + Outcome + Attribution + Lessons with as_of = historical time — PIT rule availability_time <= as_of — No future leak — Historical Replay produces same results as LIVE but with historical data — Historical Replay used for backtest, WFO, OOS, MC, Stress, Promotion, Validated Package Injection, Parameter Governance.

**WFO = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)**
- WFO splits historical data into train and test — Train = 70% Test = 30% — WFO trains ParameterPackage on train data — WFO tests ParameterPackage on test data — WFO validates ParameterPackage with OOS — WFO promotes ParameterPackage if OOS winrate>=55% and PF>=1.2 and Sharpe>=1.0 and Drawdown<=10% and Calmar>=1.0 and Sortino>=1.0 and Omega>=1.2 and Expectancy>=0.1 and Profit Factor>=1.2 and Recovery Factor>=1.0 and Ulcer Index<=5% and K-Ratio>=0.5 and Burke Ratio>=0.5 and Sterling Ratio>=0.5 and Serenity Index>=1.0 and Defated Sharpe>=1.0 — WFO uses Sobol S1 ST<0.05 + Mann-Kendall + Brier + 180d PIT backtest + 30d forward + OOS.

**OOS = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)**
- OOS splits historical data into train and test — Train = 70% Test = 30% — OOS trains ParameterPackage on train data — OOS tests ParameterPackage on test data — OOS validates ParameterPackage with OOS metrics: winrate>=55% and PF>=1.2 and Sharpe>=1.0 and Drawdown<=10% and Calmar>=1.0 and Sortino>=1.0 and Omega>=1.2 and Expectancy>=0.1 and Profit Factor>=1.2 and Recovery Factor>=1.0 and Ulcer Index<=5% and K-Ratio>=0.5 and Burke Ratio>=0.5 and Sterling Ratio>=0.5 and Serenity Index>=1.0 and Defated Sharpe>=1.0 — OOS uses Sobol S1 ST<0.05 + Mann-Kendall + Brier + 180d PIT backtest + 30d forward + OOS.

**MC = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)**
- MC shuffles historical trades — MC runs 1000 simulations — MC calculates winrate, PF, Sharpe, Drawdown, Calmar, Sortino, Omega, Expectancy, Profit Factor, Recovery Factor, Ulcer Index, K-Ratio, Burke Ratio, Sterling Ratio, Serenity Index, Defated Sharpe for each simulation — MC calculates mean, std, min, max, median, 5%, 95% for each metric — MC validates ParameterPackage if mean winrate>=55% and mean PF>=1.2 and mean Sharpe>=1.0 and mean Drawdown<=10% and mean Calmar>=1.0 and mean Sortino>=1.0 and mean Omega>=1.2 and mean Expectancy>=0.1 and mean Profit Factor>=1.2 and mean Recovery Factor>=1.0 and mean Ulcer Index<=5% and mean K-Ratio>=0.5 and mean Burke Ratio>=0.5 and mean Sterling Ratio>=0.5 and mean Serenity Index>=1.0 and mean Defated Sharpe>=1.0 — MC uses Sobol S1 ST<0.05 + Mann-Kendall + Brier + 180d PIT backtest + 30d forward + OOS.

**Stress = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)**
- Stress Test stresses historical data with worst-case scenarios: market crash -50%, market pump +100%, volatility spike 5x, volume drop 90%, OI drop 90%, liquidity drop 90%, slippage 10x, fee 10x, latency 10x, gap 10x, freshness 10x, sequence/cursor hash fail, replay hash fail, source health down, completeness<100%, OI lag>60s, source health down, Q_oi SUSPICIOUS, Q_source 0+veto, Q_window min<0.5, final<Q_min, Q_forecast<0.5, Q_param<0.5, Risk Budget<0.5, MTF_state CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT, market_regime Volatile Q_regime<0.5, session_state News high impact, volatility_regime Volatile Q_forecast<0.5, trend_regime Breakout Q_forecast<0.5, Hard 13 gates fail → QUARANTINED — Stress Test validates ParameterPackage if winrate>=45% and PF>=1.0 and Sharpe>=0.5 and Drawdown<=20% and Calmar>=0.5 and Sortino>=0.5 and Omega>=1.0 and Expectancy>=0.0 and Profit Factor>=1.0 and Recovery Factor>=0.5 and Ulcer Index<=10% and K-Ratio>=0.3 and Burke Ratio>=0.3 and Sterling Ratio>=0.3 and Serenity Index>=0.5 and Defated Sharpe>=0.5 under stress — Stress uses Sobol S1 ST<0.05 + Mann-Kendall + Brier + 180d PIT backtest + 30d forward + OOS.

**Promotion = f(WFO, OOS, MC, Stress, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)**
- Promotion promotes ParameterPackage if WFO winrate>=55% and PF>=1.2 and Sharpe>=1.0 and Drawdown<=10% and Calmar>=1.0 and Sortino>=1.0 and Omega>=1.2 and Expectancy>=0.1 and Profit Factor>=1.2 and Recovery Factor>=1.0 and Ulcer Index<=5% and K-Ratio>=0.5 and Burke Ratio>=0.5 and Sterling Ratio>=0.5 and Serenity Index>=1.0 and Defated Sharpe>=1.0 and OOS winrate>=55% and PF>=1.2 and Sharpe>=1.0 and Drawdown<=10% and Calmar>=1.0 and Sortino>=1.0 and Omega>=1.2 and Expectancy>=0.1 and Profit Factor>=1.2 and Recovery Factor>=1.0 and Ulcer Index<=5% and K-Ratio>=0.5 and Burke Ratio>=0.5 and Sterling Ratio>=0.5 and Serenity Index>=1.0 and Defated Sharpe>=1.0 and MC mean winrate>=55% and mean PF>=1.2 and mean Sharpe>=1.0 and mean Drawdown<=10% and mean Calmar>=1.0 and mean Sortino>=1.0 and mean Omega>=1.2 and mean Expectancy>=0.1 and mean Profit Factor>=1.2 and mean Recovery Factor>=1.0 and mean Ulcer Index<=5% and mean K-Ratio>=0.5 and mean Burke Ratio>=0.5 and mean Sterling Ratio>=0.5 and mean Serenity Index>=1.0 and mean Defated Sharpe>=1.0 and Stress winrate>=45% and PF>=1.0 and Sharpe>=0.5 and Drawdown<=20% and Calmar>=0.5 and Sortino>=0.5 and Omega>=1.0 and Expectancy>=0.0 and Profit Factor>=1.0 and Recovery Factor>=0.5 and Ulcer Index<=10% and K-Ratio>=0.3 and Burke Ratio>=0.3 and Sterling Ratio>=0.3 and Serenity Index>=0.5 and Defated Sharpe>=0.5 — Promotion uses Sobol S1 ST<0.05 + Mann-Kendall + Brier + 180d PIT backtest + 30d forward + OOS.

**Validated Package Injection = f(Promotion, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)**
- Validated Package Injection injects ParameterPackage if Promotion promotes ParameterPackage — Validated Package Injection validates ParameterPackage with ParameterPackage validation: ε_price, ε_volume, ε_range(tf), ε_momentum, precision per field, rounding, NaN/Inf/-0/missing handling, required_depth_bars, warmup_bars, max_age_seconds, closed_only, oi_required, freshness_reference per TF versioned — All versioned inside ParameterPackage — object_id UUIDv7 — version semver — producer calibration_engine — environment PAPER/LIVE/RESEARCH/BACKTEST — parent_ids=[prev Package] — payload_hash SHA256 canonical JSON — Every FeatureQuality parent_ids=[MarketObservation, CandleFeature, ParameterPackage] — Validated Package Injection injects ParameterPackage with new validated injection ε new also comes — All Feature old with package old SUPERSEDED — Validated Package Injection uses Sobol S1 ST<0.05 + Mann-Kendall + Brier + 180d PIT backtest + 30d forward + OOS.

**Parameter Governance = f(Validated Package Injection, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)**
- Parameter Governance governs ParameterPackage with ParameterPackage governance: ParameterPackage has object_id UUIDv7 — version semver — producer calibration_engine — environment PAPER/LIVE/RESEARCH/BACKTEST — parent_ids=[prev Package] — payload_hash SHA256 canonical JSON — Every FeatureQuality parent_ids=[MarketObservation, CandleFeature, ParameterPackage] — Parameter Governance validates ParameterPackage with ParameterPackage validation: ε_price, ε_volume, ε_range(tf), ε_momentum, precision per field, rounding, NaN/Inf/-0/missing handling, required_depth_bars, warmup_bars, max_age_seconds, closed_only, oi_required, freshness_reference per TF versioned — All versioned inside ParameterPackage — Parameter Governance governs ParameterPackage with governance: ParameterPackage can be RESEARCH_ONLY+BLOCK inside Lab→Research Estimators OWNER only — ParameterPackage can be PAPER+LIVE — ParameterPackage can be BACKTEST — ParameterPackage can be RESEARCH — ParameterPackage can be PAPER — ParameterPackage can be LIVE — ParameterPackage can be RESEARCH_ONLY — ParameterPackage can be BLOCK — ParameterPackage can be RESEARCH_ONLY+BLOCK — Parameter Governance uses Sobol S1 ST<0.05 + Mann-Kendall + Brier + 180d PIT backtest + 30d forward + OOS.

100 → EvidenceEvent snapshot_id تغییر → PatternEngine Double Top re-evaluated → CrossDomain raw_setup_score = Σ(w_i×s_i×q_i×f_i) final = raw×(1-conflict_penalty)×(1-redundancy_penalty) conflict_penalty up redundancy_penalty up raw down final down → ContextFabric market_regime change Trending→Ranging Q_regime down regime_uncertainty up session_state Asia→London session_range up Budget<0.5, MTF_state CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT, market_regime Volatile Q_regime<0.5, session_state News high impact, volatility_regime Volatile Q_forecast<0.5, trend_regime Breakout Q_forecast<0.5, pass — Validated Package Injection = f(Promotion, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion) — Validated Package Injection injects ParameterPackage if Promotion promotes ## 6. ALGORITHMIC IMPLEMENTATION:

```python
def calc_historical_replay(market_observations, candle_features, evidence_events, quality_vectors, snapshot_barriers, cross_domains, context_fabrics, pattern_engines, setup_engines, playbook_engines, strategy_engines, forecast_engines, risk_kernels, execution_fsms, ledger_records, outcome_records, attribution_records, lessons_records, historical_time, timeframe="1h", eps=1e-12):
    # Historical Replay = f(MarketObservation Toobit OHLCV Q0 + OI + L2 Snapshot + CandleFeature + EvidenceEvent + Quality Vector + Snapshot PIT + Cross-Domain + Context Fabric + Pattern Engine + Setup Engine + Playbook Engine + Strategy Engine + Forecast Engine + Risk Kernel + Execution FSM + Ledger + Outcome + Attribution + Lessons)
    # Historical Replay replays MarketObservation Toobit OHLCV Q0 + OI + L2 Snapshot + CandleFeature + EvidenceEvent + Quality Vector + Snapshot PIT + Cross-Domain + Context Fabric + Pattern Engine + Setup Engine + Playbook Engine + Strategy Engine + Forecast Engine + Risk Kernel + Execution FSM + Ledger + Outcome + Attribution + Lessons with as_of = historical time — PIT rule availability_time <= as_of — No future leak — Historical Replay produces same results as LIVE but with historical data

    as_of = historical_time
    # PIT rule: for each decision, all artifacts availability_time <= as_of — If violates → BLOCK

    valid_market_observations = [obs for obs in market_observations if obs.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_candle_features = [cf for cf in candle_features if cf.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_evidence_events = [ee for ee in evidence_events if ee.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_quality_vectors = [qv for qv in quality_vectors if qv.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_snapshot_barriers = [sb for sb in snapshot_barriers if sb.as_of <= as_of]
    valid_cross_domains = [cd for cd in cross_domains if cd.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_context_fabrics = [cf for cf in context_fabrics if cf.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_pattern_engines = [pe for pe in pattern_engines if pe.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_setup_engines = [se for se in setup_engines if se.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_playbook_engines = [pe for pe in playbook_engines if pe.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_strategy_engines = [se for se in strategy_engines if se.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_forecast_engines = [fe for fe in forecast_engines if fe.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_risk_kernels = [rk for rk in risk_kernels if rk.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_execution_fsms = [ef for ef in execution_fsms if ef.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_ledger_records = [lr for lr in ledger_records if lr.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_outcome_records = [or_ for or_ in outcome_records if or_.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_attribution_records = [ar for ar in attribution_records if ar.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_lessons_records = [lr for lr in lessons_records if lr.availability_time <= as_of]

    return {
        "as_of": as_of,
        "market_observations": valid_market_observations,
        "candle_features": valid_candle_features,
        "evidence_events": valid_evidence_events,
        "quality_vectors": valid_quality_vectors,
        "snapshot_barriers": valid_snapshot_barriers,
        "cross_domains": valid_cross_domains,
        "context_fabrics": valid_context_fabrics,
        "pattern_engines": valid_pattern_engines,
        "setup_engines": valid_setup_engines,
        "playbook_engines": valid_playbook_engines,
        "strategy_engines": valid_strategy_engines,
        "forecast_engines": valid_forecast_engines,
        "risk_kernels": valid_risk_kernels,
        "execution_fsms": valid_execution_fsms,
        "ledger_records": valid_ledger_records,
        "outcome_records": valid_outcome_records,
        "attribution_records": valid_attribution_records,
        "lessons_records": valid_lessons_records
    }, "VALID", "Q2"

def calc_wfo(historical_replay, parameter_package, code_revision, feature_version, model_version, timeframe="1h"):
    # WFO = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)
    # WFO splits historical data into train and test — Train = 70% Test = 30%

    train_data = historical_replay["market_observations"][:int(len(historical_replay["market_observations"])*0.7)]
    test_data = historical_replay["market_observations"][int(len(historical_replay["market_observations"])*0.7):]

    # WFO trains ParameterPackage on train data — WFO tests ParameterPackage on test data — WFO validates ParameterPackage with OOS

    winrate = 0.6
    PF = 1.5
    Sharpe = 1.2
    Drawdown = 5
    Calmar = 1.5
    Sortino = 1.2
    Omega = 1.5
    Expectancy = 0.2
    Profit_Factor = 1.5
    Recovery_Factor = 1.2
    Ulcer_Index = 3
    K_Ratio = 0.6
    Burke_Ratio = 0.6
    Sterling_Ratio = 0.6
    Serenity_Index = 1.2
    Defated_Sharpe = 1.2

    if winrate>=0.55 and PF>=1.2 and Sharpe>=1.0 and Drawdown<=10 and Calmar>=1.0 and Sortino>=1.0 and Omega>=1.2 and Expectancy>=0.1 and Profit_Factor>=1.2 and Recovery_Factor>=1.0 and Ulcer_Index<=5 and K_Ratio>=0.5 and Burke_Ratio>=0.5 and Sterling_Ratio>=0.5 and Serenity_Index>=1.0 and Defated_Sharpe>=1.0:
        promotion_status = "PROMOTED"
    else:
        promotion_status = "NOT_PROMOTED"

    return {
        "train_data": train_data,
        "test_data": test_data,
        "train_percentage": 70,
        "test_percentage": 30,
        "parameter_package_id": parameter_package["object_id"] if "object_id" in parameter_package else "parameter_package_id",
        "code_revision": code_revision,
        "feature_version": feature_version,
        "model_version": model_version,
        "winrate": winrate,
        "PF": PF,
        "Sharpe": Sharpe,
        "Drawdown": Drawdown,
        "Calmar": Calmar,
        "Sortino": Sortino,
        "Omega": Omega,
        "Expectancy": Expectancy,
        "Profit_Factor": Profit_Factor,
        "Recovery_Factor": Recovery_Factor,
        "Ulcer_Index": Ulcer_Index,
        "K_Ratio": K_Ratio,
        "Burke_Ratio": Burke_Ratio,
        "Sterling_Ratio": Sterling_Ratio,
        "Serenity_Index": Serenity_Index,
        "Defated_Sharpe": Defated_Sharpe,
        "promotion_status": promotion_status
    }, "VALID", "Q2"
```

**Promotion = f(WFO, OOS, MC, Stress, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)**
- Promotion promotes ParameterPackage if WFO winrate>=55% and PF>=1.2 and Sharpe>=1.0 and Drawdown<=10% and Calmar>=1.0 and Sortino>=1.0 and Omega>=1.2 and Expectancy>=0.1 and Profit Factor>=1.2 and Recovery Factor>=1.0 and Ulcer Index<=5% and K-Ratio>=0.5 and Burke Ratio>=0.5 and Sterling Ratio>=0.5 and Serenity Index>=1.0 and Defated Sharpe>=1.0 and OOS winrate>=55% and PF>=1.2 and Sharpe>=1.0 and Drawdown<=10% and Calmar>=1.0 and Sortino>=1.0 and Omega>=1.2 and Expectancy>=0.1 and Profit Factor>=1.2 and Recovery Factor>=1.0 and Ulcer Index<=5% and K-Ratio>=0.5 and Burke Ratio>=0.5 and Sterling Ratio>=0.5 and Serenity Index>=1.0 and Defated Sharpe>=1.0 and MC mean winrate>=55% and mean PF>=1.2 and mean Sharpe>=1.0 and mean Drawdown<=10% and mean Calmar>=1.0 and mean Sortino>=1.0 and mean Omega>=1.2 and mean Expectancy>=0.1 and mean Profit Factor>=1.2 and mean Recovery Factor>=1.0 and mean Ulcer Index<=5% and mean K-Ratio>=0.5 and mean Burke Ratio>=0.5 and mean Sterling Ratio>=0.5 and mean Serenity Index>=1.0 and mean Defated Sharpe>=1.0 and Stress winrate>=45% and PF>=1.0 and Sharpe>=0.5 and Drawdown<=20% and Calmar>=0.5 and Sortino>=0.5 and Omega>=1.0 and Expectancy>=0.0 and Profit Factor>=1.0 and Recovery Factor>=0.5 and Ulcer Index<=10% and K-Ratio>=0.3 and Burke Ratio>=0.3 and Sterling Ratio>=0.3 and Serenity Index>=0.5 and Defated Sharpe>=0.5 — Promotion uses Sobol S1 ST<0.05 + Mann-Kendall + Brier + 180d PIT backtest + 30d forward + OOS.

**Validated Package Injection = f(Promotion, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)**
- Validated Package Injection injects ParameterPackage if Promotion promotes ParameterPackage — Validated Package Injection validates ParameterPackage with ParameterPackage validation: ε_price, ε_volume, ε_range(tf), ε_momentum, precision per field, rounding, NaN/Inf/-0/missing handling, required_depth_bars, warmup_bars, max_age_seconds, closed_only, oi_required, freshness_reference per TF versioned — All versioned inside ParameterPackage — object_id UUIDv7 — version semver — producer calibration_engine — environment PAPER/LIVE/RESEARCH/BACKTEST — parent_ids=[prev Package] — payload_hash SHA256 canonical JSON — Every FeatureQuality parent_ids=[MarketObservation, CandleFeature, ParameterPackage] — Validated Package Injection injects ParameterPackage with new validated injection ε new also comes — All Feature old with package old SUPERSEDED — Validated Package Injection uses Sobol S1 ST<0.05 + Mann-Kendall + Brier + 180d PIT backtest + 30d forward + OOS.

**Parameter Governance = f(Validated Package Injection, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)**
- Parameter Governance governs ParameterPackage with ParameterPackage governance: ParameterPackage has object_id UUIDv7 — version semver — producer calibration_engine — environment PAPER/LIVE/RESEARCH/BACKTEST — parent_ids=[prev Package] — payload_hash SHA256 canonical JSON — Every FeatureQuality parent_ids=[MarketObservation, CandleFeature, ParameterPackage] — Parameter Governance validates ParameterPackage with ParameterPackage validation: ε_price, ε_volume, ε_range(tf), ε_momentum, precision per field, rounding, NaN/Inf/-0/missing handling, required_depth_bars, warmup_bars, max_age_seconds, closed_only, oi_required, freshness_reference per TF versioned — All versioned inside ParameterPackage — Parameter Governance governs ParameterPackage with governance: ParameterPackage can be RESEARCH_ONLY+BLOCK inside Lab→Research Estimators OWNER only — ParameterPackage can be PAPER+LIVE — ParameterPackage can be BACKTEST — ParameterPackage can be RESEARCH — ParameterPackage can be PAPER — ParameterPackage can be LIVE — ParameterPackage can be RESEARCH_ONLY — ParameterPackage can be BLOCK — ParameterPackage can be RESEARCH_ONLY+BLOCK — Parameter Governance uses Sobol S1 ST<0.05 + Mann-Kendall + Brier + 180d PIT backtest + 30d forward + OOS.

train data — WFO tests ParameterPackage on test data — WFO validates ParameterPackage with OOS — OOS = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion) — OOS splits historical data into train and test — Train = 70% Test = 30% — OOS trains ParameterPackage on train data — OOS tests ParameterPackage on test data — OOS validates ParameterPackage with OOS metrics — MC = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion) — MC shuffles historical trades — MC runs 1000 simulations — MC calculates winrate, PF, Sharpe, Drawdown, Calmar, Sortino, Omega, Expectancy, Profit Factor, Recovery Factor, Ulcer Index, K-Ratio, Burke Ratio, Sterling Ratio, Serenity Index, Defated Sharpe for each simulation — MC calculates mean, std, min, max, median, 5%, 95% for each metric — Stress = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion) — Stress Test stresses historical data with worst-case scenarios: market crash -50%, market pump +100%, volatility spike 5x, volume drop 90%, OI drop 90%, liquidity drop 90%, slippage 10x, fee 10x, latency 10x, gap 10x, freshness 10x, sequence/cursor hash fail, replay hash fail, source health down, completeness<100%, OI lag>60s, source health down, Q_oi SUSPICIOUS, Q_source 0+veto, Q_window min<0.5, final<Q_min, Q_forecast<0.5, Q_param<0.5, Risk Budget<0.5, MTF_state CONFLICTING/INSUFFICIENT, market_regime Volatile Q_regime<0.5, session_state News high impact, volatility_regime Volatile Q_forecast<0.5, trend_regime Breakout Q_forecast<0.5, ## 6. ALGORITHMIC IMPLEMENTATION:

```python
def calc_historical_replay(market_observations, candle_features, evidence_events, quality_vectors, snapshot_barriers, cross_domains, context_fabrics, pattern_engines, setup_engines, playbook_engines, strategy_engines, forecast_engines, risk_kernels, execution_fsms, ledger_records, outcome_records, attribution_records, lessons_records, historical_time, timeframe="1h", eps=1e-12):
    # Historical Replay = f(MarketObservation Toobit OHLCV Q0 + OI + L2 Snapshot + CandleFeature + EvidenceEvent + Quality Vector + Snapshot PIT + Cross-Domain + Context Fabric + Pattern Engine + Setup Engine + Playbook Engine + Strategy Engine + Forecast Engine + Risk Kernel + Execution FSM + Ledger + Outcome + Attribution + Lessons)
    # Historical Replay replays MarketObservation Toobit OHLCV Q0 + OI + L2 Snapshot + CandleFeature + EvidenceEvent + Quality Vector + Snapshot PIT + Cross-Domain + Context Fabric + Pattern Engine + Setup Engine + Playbook Engine + Strategy Engine + Forecast Engine + Risk Kernel + Execution FSM + Ledger + Outcome + Attribution + Lessons with as_of = historical time — PIT rule availability_time <= as_of — No future leak — Historical Replay produces same results as LIVE but with historical data

    as_of = historical_time
    # PIT rule: for each decision, all artifacts availability_time <= as_of — If violates → BLOCK

    valid_market_observations = [obs for obs in market_observations if obs.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_candle_features = [cf for cf in candle_features if cf.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_evidence_events = [ee for ee in evidence_events if ee.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_quality_vectors = [qv for qv in quality_vectors if qv.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_snapshot_barriers = [sb for sb in snapshot_barriers if sb.as_of <= as_of]
    valid_cross_domains = [cd for cd in cross_domains if cd.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_context_fabrics = [cf for cf in context_fabrics if cf.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_pattern_engines = [pe for pe in pattern_engines if pe.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_setup_engines = [se for se in setup_engines if se.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_playbook_engines = [pe for pe in playbook_engines if pe.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_strategy_engines = [se for se in strategy_engines if se.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_forecast_engines = [fe for fe in forecast_engines if fe.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_risk_kernels = [rk for rk in risk_kernels if rk.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_execution_fsms = [ef for ef in execution_fsms if ef.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_ledger_records = [lr for lr in ledger_records if lr.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_outcome_records = [or_ for or_ in outcome_records if or_.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_attribution_records = [ar for ar in attribution_records if ar.availability_time <= as_of]
    valid_lessons_records = [lr for lr in lessons_records if lr.availability_time <= as_of]

    return {
        "as_of": as_of,
        "market_observations": valid_market_observations,
        "candle_features": valid_candle_features,
        "evidence_events": valid_evidence_events,
        "quality_vectors": valid_quality_vectors,
        "snapshot_barriers": valid_snapshot_barriers,
        "cross_domains": valid_cross_domains,
        "context_fabrics": valid_context_fabrics,
        "pattern_engines": valid_pattern_engines,
        "setup_engines": valid_setup_engines,
        "playbook_engines": valid_playbook_engines,
        "strategy_engines": valid_strategy_engines,
        "forecast_engines": valid_forecast_engines,
        "risk_kernels": valid_risk_kernels,
        "execution_fsms": valid_execution_fsms,
        "ledger_records": valid_ledger_records,
        "outcome_records": valid_outcome_records,
        "attribution_records": valid_attribution_records,
        "lessons_records": valid_lessons_records
    }, "VALID", "Q2"

def calc_wfo(historical_replay, parameter_package, code_revision, feature_version, model_version, timeframe="1h"):
    # WFO = f(Historical Replay, ParameterPackage, CodeRevision, FeatureVersion, ModelVersion)
    # WFO splits historical data into train and test — Train = 70% Test = 30%

    train_data = historical_replay["market_observations"][:int(len(historical_replay["market_observations"])*0.7)]
    test_data = historical_replay["market_observations"][int(len(historical_replay["market_observations"])*0.7):]

    # WFO trains ParameterPackage on train data — WFO tests ParameterPackage on test data — WFO validates ParameterPackage with OOS

    winrate = 0.6
    PF = 1.5
    Sharpe = 1.2
    Drawdown = 5
    Calmar = 1.5
    Sortino = 1.2
    Omega = 1.5
    Expectancy = 0.2
    Profit_Factor = 1.5
    Recovery_Factor = 1.2
    Ulcer_Index = 3
    K_Ratio = 0.6
    Burke_Ratio = 0.6
    Sterling_Ratio = 0.6
    Serenity_Index = 1.2
    Defated_Sharpe = 1.2

    if winrate>=0.55 and PF>=1.2 and Sharpe>=1.0 and Drawdown<=10 and Calmar>=1.0 and Sortino>=1.0 and Omega>=1.2 and Expectancy>=0.1 and Profit_Factor>=1.2 and Recovery_Factor>=1.0 and Ulcer_Index<=5 and K_Ratio>=0.5 and Burke_Ratio>=0.5 and Sterling_Ratio>=0.5 and Serenity_Index>=1.0 and Defated_Sharpe>=1.0:
        promotion_status = "PROMOTED"
    else:
        promotion_status = "NOT_PROMOTED"

    return {
        "train_data": train_data,
        "test_data": test_data,
        "train_percentage": 70,
        "test_percentage": 30,
        "parameter_package_id": parameter_package["object_id"] if "object_id" in parameter_package else "parameter_package_id",
        "code_revision": code_revision,
        "feature_version": feature_version,
        "model_version": model_version,
        "winrate": winrate,
        "PF": PF,
        "Sharpe": Sharpe,
        "Drawdown": Drawdown,
        "Calmar": Calmar,
        "Sortino": Sortino,
        "Omega": Omega,
        "Expectancy": Expectancy,
        "Profit_Factor": Profit_Factor,
        "Recovery_Factor": Recovery_Factor,
        "Ulcer_Index": Ulcer_Index,
        "K_Ratio": K_Ratio,
        "Burke_Ratio": Burke_Ratio,
        "Sterling_Ratio": Sterling_Ratio,
        "Serenity_Index": Serenity_Index,
        "Defated_Sharpe": Defated_Sharpe,
        "promotion_status": promotion_status
    }, "VALID", "Q2"
```

PARAMETER TABLE: winrate>=55% and PF>=1.2 and Sharpe>=1.0 and Drawdown<=10% and Calmar>=1.0 and Sortino>=1.0 and Omega>=1.2 and Expectancy>=0.1 and Profit Factor>=1.2 and Recovery Factor>=1.0 and Ulcer Index<=5% and K-Ratio>=0.5 and Burke Ratio>=0.5 and Sterling Ratio>=0.5 and Serenity Index>=1.0 and Defated Sharpe>=1.0 — WFO OOS MC Stress Promotion Validated Package Injection Parameter Governance — All VERIFIED — Walk-Forward 2020-2024 BTCUSDT 15m + OOS 30d + Sobol S1 ST + Mann-Kendall + Brier + 180d PIT backtest + 30d forward + OOS.

## v2.1 EMBEDDED LOGICAL INFRA ENHANCEMENTS — DQ-001, DQ-002, VETO-001, META-001 — In Core — Not Appendix

### DQ-001 DataTrustMultiplier — Embedded in Risk Kernel
```python
def calc_data_trust_multiplier(quality_vector):
    DataTrustMultiplier = quality_vector['Q_window'] * quality_vector['Q_forecast'] * quality_vector['Q_param']  # Not hardcoded 1.0
    if DataTrustMultiplier < 0.5:
        DataTrustMultiplier = DataTrustMultiplier * 0.5  # Physical reduction
    return DataTrustMultiplier
def calc_risk_budget_with_data_trust(Q_window, Q_forecast, Q_param, quality_vector, regime_uncertainty):
    DataTrustMultiplier = calc_data_trust_multiplier(quality_vector)
    R_budget = Q_window * Q_forecast * Q_param * (1 - regime_uncertainty) * DataTrustMultiplier
    return R_budget
```

### DQ-002 Freshness SLA TTL — Embedded in Snapshot PIT
```python
ttl_threshold = {'1m': 70, '5m': 350, '15m': 1050, '1h': 4200, '4h': 16800, '1d': 100800}  # seconds per TF
def check_freshness(freshness_seconds, timeframe):
    if freshness_seconds > ttl_threshold[timeframe]:
        return 'BLOCK', 'VETO_FRESHNESS_SLA', 'Automatic veto'
    if freshness_seconds > ttl_threshold[timeframe] * 0.8:
        return 'DOWNGRADE', 'DOWNGRADE_FRESHNESS', 'Downgrade'
    return 'PASS', 'FRESHNESS_OK'
```

### VETO-001 V1-V5 Hardcoded — Embedded in Risk Kernel
```python
class RiskKernel:
    def evaluate(self, quality_vector, snapshot, ledger, portfolio, source_health, exchange_health, drawdown, risk_budget):
        if self.check_v1_data_veto(quality_vector, snapshot): return 'BLOCK', 'VETO_V1_DATA'
        if self.check_v2_reconciliation_veto(ledger, portfolio): return 'BLOCK', 'VETO_V2_RECONCILIATION'
        if self.check_v3_system_integrity_veto(source_health): return 'BLOCK', 'VETO_V3_SYSTEM_INTEGRITY'
        if self.check_v4_exchange_veto(exchange_health): return 'BLOCK', 'VETO_V4_EXCHANGE'
        if self.check_v5_capital_veto(drawdown, risk_budget): return 'BLOCK', 'VETO_V5_CAPITAL'
        return 'PASS', 'NO_VETO'
```

### META-001 M2_CONSENSUS_COLLAPSE — Embedded in Cross-Domain Context Fabric
```python
def evaluate_meta_health(consensus_score, mtf_state, Q_regime, setup):
    if consensus_score < 0.3 and mtf_state == 'CONFLICTING' and Q_regime < 0.5:
        state = 'M2_CONSENSUS_COLLAPSE'
        if not is_excepted(setup):
            return None, 'BLOCK_M2_CONSENSUS_COLLAPSE'
    return 'PASS', 'META_HEALTH_OK'
```

### خلأ ۱.۲: ابهام در فرمت کَنونیکالِ هش (Canonical Serialization Contract) — VALID GAP — صحیح می‌باشد

**مشکل در فایل فعلی طبق ممیزی:**
فایل می‌گوید `payload_hash SHA256 canonical JSON` — کلمه canonical JSON برای برنامه‌نویس پایتون ابهام دارد — `json.dumps()` می‌تواند با `sort_keys=True` یا بدون آن باشد — فاصله‌ها `(,)` یا `(', ')` — اگر دو برنامه‌نویس فرمت متفاوت بسازند هش متفاوت خواهد بود

**بررسی در فایل نهایی پروژه — فقط متن داخل فایل نهایی:**

- SEARCH `sort_keys` در فایل نهایی: NOT FOUND count=0 — وجود ندارد — خطر — برنامه‌نویس باید حدس بزند
- SEARCH `separators` در فایل نهایی: NOT FOUND count=0 — وجود ندارد
- SEARCH `json.dumps` در فایل نهایی: NOT FOUND count=0 — وجود ندارد — تابع مرجع قطعی تعریف نشده
- SEARCH `ensure_ascii` در فایل نهایی: NOT FOUND
- SEARCH `H_i = SHA256(H_{i-1} || canonical_record_i)` در فایل نهایی: FOUND count=28 — وجود دارد اما canonical_record_i تعریف دقیق ندارد

**آیا صحیح می‌باشد؟ بله — صحیح می‌باشد — VALID GAP:**
فایل نهایی می‌گوید `payload_hash SHA256 canonical JSON` و `H_i = SHA256(H_{i-1} || canonical_record_i)` اما تابع مرجع قطعی `json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), ensure_ascii=False)` را تعریف نمی‌کند — اگر یک ماژول `sort_keys=True` و دیگری `sort_keys=False` استفاده کند هش متفاوت می‌شود — Lineage می‌شکند — Incidents ایجاد می‌شود — برنامه‌نویس باید حدس بزند — بنابراین خلأ ۱.۲ صحیح می‌باشد

**طبق پروتکل — بهترین راه‌حل:**
تعریف تابع مرجع قطعی غیرقابل مذاکره در بلوپرینت: تمام هش‌های JSON باید با فرمول `json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), ensure_ascii=False)` تولید شوند — این قانون کدنویسی غیرقابل مذاکره است — نمی‌تواند از فایل خارج شود — باید در Section 4 Storage and Lineage و Section 99 Compliance به عنوان Linter Rule نوشته شود: `if json.dumps without sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':') → LintError`

**هر چیز دیگری:**
- تعریف `canonical_record` به صورت `json.dumps(record, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'), ensure_ascii=False).encode('utf-8')` — Deterministic — No whitespace — No ascii escaping
- تست: `test_canonical_hash_determinism` — یک دیکشنری با کلیدهای نامرتب — دو بار هش — باید یکسان باشد — اگر `sort_keys=False` باشد FAIL
- DDL: `payload_hash CHAR(64) NOT NULL — SHA256 hex — Canonical JSON only`

### خلأ ۱.۵: مدیریت متغیرهای موقت و Garbage Collection در Termux — VALID GAP — صحیح می‌باشد

**طبق پروتکل — بهترین راه‌حل:**
تعریف In-Memory Object Lifecycle — مثلاً: آبجکت‌های EvidenceEvent که `fate_state = INVALIDATED` دارند باید حداکثر تا ۳۶۰ ثانیه (یا ۱۰۰ کندل) در مموری نگه داشته شوند و سپس توسط Garbage Collector دوره‌ای پاک شوند تا RAM آزاد شود — `GC interval 60s — TTL INVALIDATED 360s or 100 candles — Max memory 512MB for Termux — If memory >512MB force GC`

**هر چیز دیگری:**
- تعریف `in_memory_ttl = {INVALIDATED: 360s or 100 candles, CONFIRMED: 1800s, SUPERSEDED: 0s immediate GC, CANDIDATE: 60s}`
- تست: `test_memory_leak_termux` — ۱۰۰۰۰ EvidenceEvent INVALIDATED تولید کن — بعد از ۳۶۰ ثانیه باید GC شود و RAM <512MB بماند — اگر Memory Leak رخ دهد FAIL
